**Previous update: 2026-09-14 (E63 addendum B.8 — RETRACTION of B.7/§62.11) — THE BYTE AXIS IS NOT CHEAP: AT HALF A BYTE IT IS THE MOST EXPENSIVE TREATMENT IN THE PROGRAMME (82% OF THE DISTANCE TO CHANCE), AND THE CARVE IS THE SMALL HALF (17%).** B.7 priced activation against E37's `3.475706` — **which is not the dense model but the model with its FFN ALREADY TERNARISED**, i.e. already at half a byte. On the one baseline no treatment has moved (fp32 dense **0.767595**, chance **4.069819**, gap **3.302224**): **half a byte = +2.708112 = 82.01%**, **carve to 1.17% = +0.553692 = 16.77%**, **one byte (int8) = +0.0012525 = 0.038%**. **The ranking reverses** — B.7's “93.20%” was large only because its denominator was small, and it was small because ternarisation had already spent 82% of it. **That is the floor rule (E59 §8), cited in the same document that broke it**, and the **third** search-rule miss of the day: `BRIEF_H1` — whose §1 heading is *“What is actually broken — and it is NOT mostly the carve”* — came back in my own grep output **twice** and I did not open it. **What survives is larger than what was withdrawn:** the byte axis has a **CLIFF** (E60, between 1 B and half a byte) — cheapest treatment measured above it, most expensive below it, so *“bytes are cheap” is a property of one side of a cliff, not of the axis* — and **H1 should train at ONE byte, not half**, because at one byte the ternary term, **82% of the +3.22 BPB H1 must heal**, never exists. **The cheap unmeasured step this exposes:** the carve's +0.553692 was measured **on a ternary FFN**; its cost **on an int8 FFN** was unmeasured at this point — BPB only, no timing, no GPU. **`G-E63d` remains `VOID` and OWED; nothing here is a rate.** (brief addendum B.8, ledger §62.12)

**2026-09-14 (E63 addendum B.6 — a SECOND correction, and it reverses which problem is open) — SPEED AT TEN BILLION IS NOT THE BLOCKER: A GENUINE 10 B ABOVE 100 tok/s WITH A LIVE FFN WAS MEASURED IN E40, AND E38 HAD ALREADY CLOSED THE ROUTE TO FILLING IT.** B.5 corrected B.4 by opening E39 and **stopped one experiment short**. E40 — titled `ATTENTION-LEVERS-EXHAUSTED` — pulled every remaining attention lever: **`R128`** (`NKV` 8→2, rank 128, `F=49152`, untied, the same 9,999,220,736 parameters) reads **112.73 / 106.44 tok/s** at `k=3`, quiet-box re-readings **128.54 / 123.33 / 130.30**, so per §53.9.4 **the quantity is ~113–130 tok/s**, floor **~161–165**. Floor ladder `A10B` 58.1 → `R512` ~93 → `NKV2` ~127 → **`R128` ~163**. **B.5 had cited the weaker number** — E39's 99.4/100.0 floor is **FFN-OFF by construction** (E40: `R512` reaches 100 only at `k*≈0`), whereas `R128` clears EXCELLENT **with the FFN computing**. **So B.4's “next experiment is attention” was wrong THREE ways** — measured (E39), then exhausted (E40), and quoted off the FFN-off floor — **and the argument was moot anyway:** E38 `SELECTION-IS-DEAD` handed the carve a **perfect, per-token, unattainable oracle** at the 1.17% activation this speed requires and it read **4.131817 BPB** against dense **0.767595** and chance **4.069819** — **above the chance line**, and no router beats its own oracle. **The open problem at ten billion is QUALITY at the activation rate speed requires, and the only route left — training with the mask ON — is H1, already launched.** E63's one byte per weight stays worth having as orthogonal fidelity headroom at −1.7% of rate; it is not the blocker. **New search law:** when a search returns a **series**, read to the **END** of it — B.5 was a correction for not searching that repeated the error one experiment later; *opening the first hit on an axis is a sample, not a search.* **E40's weights are NOISE, its dispersion is poor, and `G-E63d` remains `VOID` and OWED — the goal is NOT claimed.** (brief addendum B.6, `probes/E63…` §14, ledger §62.10)

**2026-09-14 (E63 addendum B.5 — a CORRECTION I owe out loud) — I NAMED ATTENTION AS “THE NEXT EXPERIMENT” FOR AN EXPERIMENT E39 HAD ALREADY RUN, AND WON.** Addendum B.4 closed with *“the deficit is not closable on the FFN axis, and the next experiment is attention, not experts”* — **written without opening E39.** `A10B-R512`, the same 9,999,220,736 parameters with `q_proj`/`o_proj` as rank-512 factors and the freed weight moved into the FFN, reads **78.456 tok/s** (registered run 1; run 2's 80.548 may not promote it), **1.693× within session** over matched-parameter `A10B`, with a fitted **attention+head+router floor of 10.059 / 9.996 ms = 99.4 / 100.0 tok/s** — the two runs agreeing to **0.62%** where the slope disagrees by 10.4%, against E34's 20.03 tok/s floor at T10. Its planted control fired (rank 4096 moves 58% more weight and was slower in both runs). **No B.4 arithmetic is withdrawn and the correction makes it STRONGER** — the 72.3% attention share is the *mechanism* for E39's 1.69×. This is `feedback_search_before_claiming_a_gap` in its milder form: not *“nobody measured this”* but *“this is what to measure next”*, about something already measured — **a statement about the programme's conduct passes through no gate.** What it opens is narrower than B.4 implied: recomputed from the apparatus' own `active_weights()`, `A10B-R512` is attention **50.62%**, head **25.31%**, router 3.16%, **carved FFN 20.91%** against `A10B-K3`'s 72.30 / 14.46 / 1.81 / **11.44** — so its one-byte charged-byte ratio is **1.2091** (vs 1.1144) and **E63's measured 1.7% does NOT transfer to it.** That shape must be measured, not extrapolated. **Apparatus defect found in passing and fixed:** `synth_export.py:439` never forwarded `rank` to the sidecar's `total_weights`, so every ranked artefact reports the dense-q/o count — `e39_r512` **+4.03%**, `e39_r4096` **−4.91%**, **wrong in both directions because the field is constant in `rank`**; `G-E39A` passes the rank itself and is untouched, so **the gate was right and the metadata beside it was wrong.** **`G-E63d` remains `VOID` and OWED — nothing here is a rate, and the goal is not claimed.** (brief addendum B.5, `probes/E63…` §13, `probes/E39…` §0, ledger §62.9)

**2026-09-14 (E63 INTERIM) — ONE BYTE PER WEIGHT COSTS THE CARVED 10 B ABOUT **1.7%** OF ITS RATE, NOT THE 27% §60.4 ASSUMED — AND §60.4 IS THEREFORE PESSIMISTIC, NOT OPTIMISTIC.** Registered in brief addendum C **before the run**, with a non-promotion clause. **`G-E63d` is `VOID` and OWED** — all 18 cells sat over `OCC_BAR` (4.95–22.75%, the load is the user's browser), so the absolute rate was not taken and is not claimed. What a contended box CAN answer is the **paired** ratio, 9 interleaved reps per arm: **int8 ÷ packed = 0.9900, CI [0.9106, 1.0228]**, arms asymmetric by 2.82 points in int8's favour so **corrected to 0.9826**. The control that makes it quotable: E36 measured this packed artefact at **49.96 tok/s clean**, E52's `k` predicts **48.19** at this sweep's 13.51% foreign, and the arm read **47.22 — 2.0% apart.** Composed with E36's clean absolute — **an estimate with both inputs named, not a measurement** — the carved 10 B cell at one byte per weight sits at **≈49 tok/s (CI [45.5, 51.1])**. **My registered prediction 5 (0.60–0.72) is 36% below this and prediction 4 (32–36 tok/s) is heading for a bad miss** — both stay UNSCORED until the clean sweep, by addendum C.3. **The mechanism was registered in advance (addendum B): §60.4 priced the format change as though it applied to the token, and it applies to 11.4% of it** — attention is **72.3%** of the A10B-K3 charged token, head 14.5%, router 1.8%, and all three stay at half a byte in both artefacts. A free FFN would buy at most ×1.129. The measured ratio is **above even the charged-byte comparator (0.8974)**, so the engine is not purely bandwidth-bound on the carved FFN and **E31's 64-weight block does not cost what its byte count says**. **The goal is NOT met: one clean hour turns a ≈49 estimate into a measurement.** `probes/E63_THE_TEN_BILLION_CELL_AT_ONE_BYTE.md` §12, `SPEED_LEDGER` §62.7–62.8.

**2026-09-14 (E63 PART A) — THE 10 B CELL AT ONE BYTE PER WEIGHT NOW EXISTS AND ITS ARITHMETIC IS EXACT; THE RATE IS STILL OWED.** E62 made the measured 10 B cell the only route, and the engine refused to load one: `donor_engine.c:477` *“row-selected matvec is implemented for the plain packed kind only”*. E63 built the path — two additive matrix kinds (`MK_I8`, `MK_I8_T`), a row list threaded through the int8 fallback, and an int8 branch of `matvec_colacc` that is **simpler** than the packed kernel (no trit decode, no `pshufb`). **`G-E63a` FIRES**: five existing artefacts generate **byte-identical ids AND bit-identical prefill logits** on the new binary. **`G-E63b` FIRES** at both shapes with **0.00% agreement** between `k=3` and `k=E` (max |d| 4.097) — the fidelity counter has range in the direction that fails. **`G-E63c` EQUIVALENT**: the int8 carve reproduces the packed carve at **100.0000% top-1 on every cell**, worst max |d| **1.444e-05**, 69× inside the 1e-3 bar. **The artefact is real:** `e63_a10b_i8.bin`, 10,015,507,256 B, Gate V3 clean at 195 tensors. **Two of the brief's own gates could not be ANSWERED as written** (an int8-dense synthetic is not expressible — the engine refuses a mixed FFN; and the standard slice's ids exceed A10B's V=32768) — re-specified in **addendum A, pushed before the run**, E4's malformed-gate precedent. **Predictions 3 of 4 answerable; prediction 6 MISSED** (10.016 GB against a registered 10.9 ± 0.2 — I doubled the whole file when only the FFN changes width; 17.3% of it is attention, head and embeddings). **NO RATE: §60.4's 36.6–37.6 tok/s is untouched and is still a desk model.** Part B (`G-E63d`, `G-E63e`) is written, frozen and **OWED on an idle box**. `probes/E63_THE_TEN_BILLION_CELL_AT_ONE_BYTE.md`, `SPEED_LEDGER` §62.

**2026-09-14 (E62) — THE INT8 DAMAGE DOES NOT RANK WITH SCALE, SO THE 10 B HEADLINE'S FIDELITY LEG CANNOT BE EXTRAPOLATED AT ALL — ONLY MEASURED.** §60.4's 10 B headline (36.6–37.6 tok/s, 73–75% of the bar) is a SPEED claim resting on a QUALITY assumption nobody had tested: that one byte per weight is still faithful at 10 B. Two points existed and they fitted either `N^2.578` (≈0.15 BPB at 10 B, headline void) or linear in N (≈0.008, rung fine) — a **~19× spread at the target**. E62 measured Qwen2.5-3B and got both an answer and the loss of the question. **dBPB(3 B) = 0.000579 = 0.116 σ_seed**, inside the pre-registered `AT-MOST-LINEAR` band (≤0.0025): **the explosion is not happening.** But the damage is **NOT MONOTONE IN N** — 6.64e-05 → **1.25e-03** → 5.79e-04, with the **1.5 B donor the worst of the three, 2.16× the 3 B** — so `G-E62d` clause 1 breaks and, by the rule registered before the run, **the score is reported WITHOUT a trend claim.** *A law was fitted through two adjacent points and the third point refuted the law's EXISTENCE, not its exponent.* The three-point fit (`N^1.321`) is printed only to show it is meaningless. **Net epistemic position: better on the headline, WORSE on the basis** — the strongest reason to think the rung dies at 10 B is gone, and so is any right to extrapolate; **the 10 B cell must be measured.** Controls: `G-E62a` FIRES (`05b F32` reproduces E60 at **+0.00e+00** on a box 13.5% foreign-occupied; `05b I8` at +3.27e-09, which is E61's `m1`→`m4` accumulator delta **to the last digit**); `G-E62b` ADMISSIBLE at **1.11e-05** against E12's PyTorch fp32, *in family* with 1.53e-05 and 1.14e-05 at the other two scales. E12's owed 3 B packed R3 cell paid: **4.234751, ABOVE the 4.069819 chance line again** after dipping below at 1.5 B — non-monotone too, in the OPPOSITE direction, so **1.5 B is the extreme at both formats and they disagree about the sign**. **2.5 of 6 predictions, the worst record in this series: every prediction about the physics was wrong.** No speed claim, no T4 ask. `probes/E62_DOES_THE_ONE_BYTE_RUNG_SURVIVE_SCALE.md`, `SPEED_LEDGER` §61.

**2026-09-14 (E61) — THE CHAIN WAS NOT THE BINDER, AND E61's OWN PLANTED CONTROL KILLED E61's MECHANISM.** E8 wired `--mvacc` into two of `matvec_sel`'s three weight branches in 2026-09-07 and skipped the third, writing down why: *"not on the measured path, so not urgent."* E60 made it the measured path — it is the int8 rung, the only artefact that is both above the 50 tok/s bar and faithful — and the assembly still read one unbroken `ymm0→ymm1→ymm0` chain, 4 FMAs per 32 weight bytes. Latency arithmetic said the branch sat at **87–91% of its own 36.4 GB/s chain ceiling** where fp32 sits at 6.5% of its and packed at 30% of its, and predicted **×1.12–1.22** from breaking it. **Measured: `05b` 65.13 → 68.52 tok/s = ×1.0507, REFUTED against the registered 1.06 floor; `15b` 20.61 → 22.56 = ×1.0662, UNDECIDED with a paired CI straddling 1.0.** The debt was worth ~5%, not 19%. **The planted positive is what refuted the mechanism, not the treatment:** `PACKED` sits at **90.5% of ITS single-chain ceiling too** and gained **×1.2745** — two kernels at the same fraction of their own latency wall, one gaining 27% and the other 5%, so *fraction-of-chain-ceiling predicts nothing*. What won was the alternative the brief ranked LAST: **a 1 B/weight stream genuinely binds near 34–35 GB/s on this box.** So E60's three-bandwidth curve, re-measured with EVERY kernel at four chains — the exact criticism E61 was launched to make of it — survives: **37.64 / 33.97–34.90 / 20.65 GB/s at 4 / 1 / 0.5 B per weight**. `SPEED_LEDGER` §59.2's middle denominator is **corrected upward (31.7–33.1 → 34.0–34.9) and NOT withdrawn**; E61 set out to undermine §59 and ended up load-bearing for it. Kept anyway, because it is free: `05b` **68.52 tok/s** and `15b` **22.56** at **100.0000% per-position top-1 over 12,264 positions** and dBPB ~3e-09 — and `--mvacc 1` is sha256-identical to `donor_engine_e60.exe`, so every E60 number reproduces. **The fidelity metric E60 owed is now built and in service** (`--bpb --top1`), with its own ceiling control: `F32` twice = 12264/12264, `PACKED` vs `F32` = **281/12264 = 2.29%**. **Two failures recorded:** the null control read **1.0947**, missing refutation of the mechanism by 0.0053, on cells at **19.57% foreign, 9/9 over `OCC_BAR`** — owed a repeat on an idle box, not a re-run to a pass; and E60's 1.98 tok/s value-dependence **did not replicate** (+0.68 → +0.88 on an arm with 8.5 tok/s of spread), so that question reopens. **4.5 of 7 predictions.** Chain-latency derivations are now **0 for 2 on magnitude** (E8 predicted 1.5–1.7, measured 1.349) and 2 for 2 on direction. 10 B desk model, noise weights: faithful arm **36.6–37.6 tok/s = 73–75% of the bar**, gap **×1.33–1.37**. Still not the goal. `probes/E61_THE_CHAIN_ON_THE_BYTE_THAT_WORKS.md`

**2026-09-14 (E60) — THE PRECISION CLIFF IS BETWEEN 1 BYTE AND HALF A BYTE, NOT BETWEEN 4 BYTES AND 1. AND THE BYTE LADDER IS NOT A BANDWIDTH LADDER.** The engine had two rungs, 4 B/weight and 0.5 B/weight, and fifty-nine experiments concluding that the first is faithful and slow and the second is fast and destroyed. **Both halves are true and neither is about quantization — they are about a rung nobody built.** `matvec_sel`'s `quant==1` fallback was already a general int8×fp32 dot with a per-row scale, so the middle rung needed an EXPORTER, not a kernel. **`05b_i8h` reads 63.87 tok/s (CI 61.76–64.01) at dBPB +0.000066 — the first trained artifact this programme has put ABOVE the 50 tok/s bar whose modelling cost is 1.3% of one seed sigma**, ×3.35 over the faithful fp32 arm's 19.08. `15b_i8h` reads 21.39 at +0.001252 (×3.41, below the bar). **The first 4× byte cut is free; the next 2× costs 2,162–55,096× more and lands the model ABOVE CHANCE.** Both planted controls fired before any treatment number was read (`e60` ≡ frozen `e53` 184/184 ×4; the fidelity instrument reproduced E57's 160/3/160/10 exactly; the BPB instrument replicated E1 to 4.47e-09). **The second finding outranks the first because it corrects arithmetic used everywhere else: ONE BOX HAS THREE BANDWIDTHS** — 37.7–38.8 GB/s at 4 B/weight, **31.7–33.1 at 1 B (84–85%)**, **21.5–22.7 at 0.5 B (57–59%)** — and `rate ratio = byte ratio × bandwidth-efficiency ratio` closes at all four cells to **0.4%**. No projection may divide one format's bytes by another format's bandwidth; E58 §8's *"a bound is a number with a scope"* now has a measured curve instead of a caution. On E36's registered 10 B cell this takes the FAITHFUL arm from 10.2 to **34–36 tok/s (68–71% of the bar)** — **the faithful-to-bar gap goes ×4.9 → ×1.4** (desk model, noise weights). **It does NOT reach the goal:** a dense 10 B at 1 B/weight reads ~3.3 tok/s, so on the goal's own shape int8 is STRICTLY WORSE than ternary. **4.5 of 6 predictions; both misses cost me an argument I had written into the brief** — `G-E60c` UNRESOLVABLE refutes "speed is a function of bytes, not values", and `G-E60e` DEGRADED is left standing while being named DEFECTIVE (its two clauses disagree by 301×/16× because a 160-token greedy count absorbs). (`probes/E60_THE_RUNG_THAT_WAS_NEVER_BUILT.md`, ledger §59)

**2026-09-13 (E59) — THE FASTEST KERNEL THIS PROGRAMME HAS IS WORTH ×1.253 / ×1.333 ON TRAINED WEIGHTS AND IT CHANGES 42–74% OF THE TOKENS.** E58 addendum A left one engine-side lever — `Rem` is 3.7%/2.0% of the token, so only a faster weight kernel can move a ternary arm — and the binary already ships one. **E59 measured it on the real trained artifacts, speed and fidelity in the same table, for the first time since `--lutblk` was written.** Speed: `05b_tqh` **85.32 → 106.88 tok/s (×1.253)**, `15b_tqh` **28.87 → 38.48 (×1.333)**, bootstrap intervals **SEPARATED**, reproducing E13's ×1.217/×1.358 on different weights. Fidelity, paired against the packed path on the **same bytes**: **41/160 = 25.6%** and **55/160 = 34.4%** agreement, diverging at **token 0** on 8 of 10 prompts. `--lut-group 32` is a **Pareto point nobody had measured** — it costs **1.4%/0.3%** of speed and buys **+16.3/+23.1 points** of agreement (41.9% / 57.5%). `G-E59a` FIRES on both sides: `--lut` ≡ `--lutblk` **160/160**, reproducing E13's sha256 identity on trained weights, and `--lutblk` ≠ packed by 119 and 105 tokens. **The methodological result outranks the rates:** scoring these arms against HuggingFace — the obvious test — reads **3/160→4/160** and **10/160→10/160**, i.e. *"the kernel costs nothing"*, and it is false by 42–74 points. **A counter on its FLOOR cannot show damage** — `feedback_gate_is_not_a_progress_meter` inverted, and the dangerous half. Refusing that column the verdict **before** the run is the only reason E59 has an answer. **4.5 of 6 predictions; both misses say the kernel is MORE destructive than E14's anchor.** What is left: every number here is int8 activations applied **post hoc**, and post-hoc is what this programme keeps measuring as fatal and training as survivable — so `H2T` should heal against the activation quantiser too, at **zero extra GPU hours** (`decisions/T4_HEALING_PROPOSAL.md` §13.6). (`probes/E59_THE_FAST_KERNEL_DOES_NOT_SURVIVE.md`, ledger §58)

**2026-09-13 (E58 + addendum A) — THE **PACKED** KERNEL HAS ×1.11–×1.17 LEFT, NOT ×1.75 — AND THE BINARY SHIPS A FASTER ONE THE PROBE FORGOT TO SAY IT WASN'T USING.** **CORRECTION (addendum A, same evening):** E58's first headline read *"the engine has ×1.11–×1.17 left"* and that is **false**. Every ceiling it measures is the **packed** kernel's; the same binary carries **`--lutblk`**, which E13 measured **end-to-end** at **×1.217** (0.5 B, `--bench 300`) and **×1.358** (Coder-7B), and at kernel level **67.51–103.05 G-w/s** against E10's best packed cell of 52.95. The scope was in E58's tables and died on the way to its own headline: fourth tally on `feedback_exact_tables_overstating_prose`. E57's addendum D published ×1.75 of engine headroom on the ternary path and — the number that mattered — **47.71 tok/s for a healed 1.5 B, "95% of the good bar"**. **Both are WITHDRAWN.** They divide a *packed-kernel* rate by the *fp32* proj-GEMV floor of 37.0 GB/s, a bound `SPEED_LEDGER` §23.3 had **already withdrawn for the packed path on 2026-09-07** (E10: that kernel is `CORE-BOUND` at ~25.5 GB/s / 49.11–52.95 G-weights/s, flat to 8% across a 512× footprint change). E58 opens the organs of both trained ternary artifacts with `--profile`: **`G-E58a` closes at +0.24% and +0.35%** against a ±8% bar, so the attribution is readable, and the weight path runs at **44.94 and 46.46 G-w/s — 85–88% of the best cell E10 ever measured**, with `head`, `down` and `gate+up` *inside* E10's band. **`Rem` — attention, rope, norms, glue, sampling — is 0.427 ms (3.7%) and 0.668 ms (2.0%) of the token, eleven times smaller than the gap it would have had to fill**, so the missing multiple is not in the glue either: it was never a quantity. Honest ceilings: **87.58 → 102.50 tok/s (×1.17)** at 0.5 B and **29.51 → 33.54 (×1.14)** at 1.5 B. Turned around, 50 tok/s at the 1.5 B shape needs **33.7% fewer streamed weights** — **1.024 G active ternary weights/token, inside E18 §31's independently derived 0.982–1.060 G**. The laggard is `qkv_proj` at both scales (the registered prediction, held twice) and fixing it entirely buys 2.0%. **4 of 6 predictions held; both misses said there is LESS headroom than I thought.** (`probes/E58_WHERE_DID_THE_1_75x_GO.md`, ledger §57)

**2026-09-13 (E57) — A TRAINED LLM IS FAITHFUL ON THIS ENGINE AT 19.09 tok/s AND BROKEN AT EVERY FASTER SETTING.** Fifty-six experiments produced every tok/s on **synthetic** weights; E57 measured the six **real trained Qwen2.5** arms that have sat on this disk since E6. **`G-E57a` fires** — all three E6 controls reproduce exactly seven days on (160/160, 3/160, 10/160). **CORRECTION (addendum B, same day): the quality half of E57 is a REPLICATION, not a discovery — E17 scored these exact arms on 2026-09-07 and every one of the seven readings matches to the token** (`15b_f32` 160/160, `15b_tq` 12/160, `05b_tq` 3/160, and the twin comparisons 81/160 and 28/160). This brief's §3 claimed they had "never been scored"; that was **false and contradicted by a file in `results/`**. What is new is the SPEED table, which E17 §7 explicitly refused to produce, and the joint gate. The replication is worth having on its own terms — **seven readings bit-stable across two engine builds** (`donor_engine_e13` → `donor_engine_e53`, i.e. across E49's avx4 kernel and E53's vexpf8) **and six days** — but it is a parity result, not a finding. **And my registered prediction was not merely wrong, it was contradicted by this repository at the moment I wrote it:** I predicted the ternary *body* was survivable and the *head* broke A2; the **body alone diverges at token 0** (`05b_tq` **3/160**, `15b_tq` **12/160**), and ternarising the head on top changes the count by 0 at 0.5B and 2 at 1.5B. The two 0.5B arms returned the same count *and* the same divergence positions — the shape a code-path defect makes — so they were re-generated and compared to each other: **0 of 5 sequences identical, 28 of 160 tokens equal**. Different models, coincident counts. At 1.5B the two ternary arms agree with **each other** on 81/160 while agreeing with HuggingFace on 12 and 10. **`G-E57d`: NO ARM DEMONSTRATES THE TARGET** — 90 cells, bootstrap intervals: FAITHFUL and slow `05b_f32` **19.09** / `15b_f32` **6.25**; FAST and broken `05b_tqh` **83.41** / `05b_tq` **43.69** / `15b_tqh` **29.18** / `15b_tq` **19.15**. The fastest faithful arm is **38% of the 50 tok/s bar**; the fastest arm is 4.4× faster and emits 3 of 160 correct tokens. **The programme's headline rate has been standing on the broken side of that table.** Two instruments moved too: **`G-E55a2` is MALFORMED** — it refused all six arms on IQRs of 0.56–2.29%, tighter than the 1.43% and 2.34% windows E55 *passed*, and a planted control on the gate itself (`e57_g55a2_control.py`, 300 draws × 4 true dispersions spanning 30×) shows P(PASS) is **22–28% at k=15 in every row** and 93–94% at k=40: the verdict is a function of the rep count and blind to the dispersion it gates. E57 run 1 is **not** re-labelled (E36 run-2 rule) and the conclusion does not depend on it (19.09 against 50 is a factor of 2.6). And **E56 arrives over-determined**: foreign occupancy anti-correlates with rate across arms, **r = −0.651** — `15b_f32` cleared `OCC_BAR` on **0 of 15** cells on an idle box while the fastest arm cleared 10 of 15, so the bar is biased against exactly the memory-bound arms. **Brief: `briefs/BRIEF_E57_WHAT_DOES_A_TRAINED_LLM_ACTUALLY_COST_ON_THIS_ENGINE.md` addenda A–C.** **ADDENDUM C, zero new compute:** the continuous partner E14 §3 demands was already published in E1 and it **reclassifies the finding** — at 0.5B the ternary model scores **4.509 BPB against a chance line of 4.070**, i.e. **ABOVE chance**, so `3/160` is not "very lossy", it is **not a model**; at 1.5B the same conversion lands 0.586 **below** chance and does predict, badly. **The body is the organ on the continuous metric too**: `tq`→`tqh` costs +0.022 BPB at 0.5B and **−0.0085 at 1.5B, where ternarising the head IMPROVES BPB.** **C.7 then corrects C.4 the same way B corrected §3**: `BRIEF_E12_TERNARY_COST_VS_SCALE` asks the scale question already, ran over 0.5B/1.5B/**3B**, and returned `CONTROL-FAILED` with §9 suspending even that — I wrote the paragraph from a filename in a listing without opening it. The two readable same-family pairs **disagree in sign** (damage −23.5% on R3, +27.5% on BitLinear158), so **no scale argument is available to this programme in either direction**, and the cheapest hope for a fast 10B is closed on evidence. **Consequence, in `decisions/T4_HEALING_PROPOSAL.md` §13: the next GPU arm is `H2T` (heal the ternary BODY at 1.5B, 2×2.8 h, judged on BPB and free-running), and the literal 10B clause is blocked by MEMORY not hours — a T4 is 16 GB and a 10B in training wants ~120 GB, so no number of weeks buys it.**

**2026-09-13 (notte, E53 run 1, quality) — A VECTORISED EXPONENTIAL REPRODUCES THIS ENGINE'S GREEDY OUTPUT TOKEN FOR TOKEN, AND THE SPEED IS STILL UNMEASURED BECAUSE THE BOX CANNOT MEET ITS OWN BAR.** `vexpf8`: AVX2, 8 lanes, Cody-Waite reduction, Cephes degree-5 minimax, `2^n` applied as two halves in sequence — wired to **both** sites (attention softmax and SwiGLU) behind **one** flag `--fexp {libm,poly}`, with `CONFIG fexp=...` printed always (E50's law). **`G-E53a` FIRES** (degree-2 control 661 ulp, 56× the bar). **`G-E53b2` PASS: 1 ulp EVERYWHERE**, 1.1920e-07 relative on normals, denormals within 1 ulp, zero/±inf/NaN/specials exact — and **`G-E53b` as written was MALFORMED**, because at `x = −104` the two representable neighbours of `exp(x)` are `+0` and one denormal ulp, **100% and 106% off**, so no implementation including `expf` itself could ever have met it (E4 precedent; the replacement is **eight times stricter**, not looser). **`c0` IDENTICAL**: the refactor of the softmax loop and the three SwiGLU loops reproduces `NATS_TOTAL 124963.9517608703`, the published pre-E53 value, **every digit** — so the control arm is still the control arm. **`G-E53c1` PARITY HOLDS at 5.697e-08** relative over 12,264 positions (control fires at 1.593e-01). **`G-E53c2` PASS: A1 160/160**, planted controls **3/160** and **10/160** reproducing E6 to the token, `G-D` 15 of 15 — **and that is the gate that could have broken**: bit-exact parity was impossible by two reasons registered in advance (a minimax polynomial is a different approximation; an 8-lane horizontal sum is a different summation order), so a discrete argmax had to be earned, scored against HF and not against my own libm arm. **THE SPEED PHASE: THREE GATES, THREE REFUSALS, AND NOTHING FROM IT IS CITABLE.** `G-E53e` **DRIFT-CONTAMINATED** (bracketing witnesses 5.68% apart against a 2.3% rule), **all 50 cells above `OCC_BAR = 4.39`** (foreign 4.9–23.8%), `G-E53d` **C50 NOT COMPUTABLE** (1 of 5 windows survived the 6% dispersion rule). **Measured afterwards rather than guessed: the box at rest with no engine running reads 5.6% foreign, of which CHROME ALONE IS ~4.2%.** E52 addendum A.6 predicted the derived bar would be one the machine could not meet, and B.2 scored that WRONG; **E52 B.7 now carries the correction and run 2 becomes 3 right / 4 wrong.** The bar is not re-opened (E40 addendum A) — a quiet box is asked of the user in `COMMUNICATION.md` item 1. **Two diagnostics, labelled, carrying no verdict**: (a) the attention softmax pass goes **1.6530 → 0.0540 ms** (−96.7%), `glue(silu)` 0.0900 → 0.0380 ms, token 13.5099 → 12.0106 ms — and **1.6530 reproduces E51's 1.6890 to −2.1%** on a different build, session and runner; the projection `b` 0.008144 → 0.005646 and `C50` 1443 → 2082 is written down only so it is not hidden, and rests on a contaminated session; (b) **the two arms' five repetitions are COMPLETELY DISJOINT at n = 320, 640 and 1280** (worst-case gaps +2.18%, +2.76%, +12.01%) while overlapping at 40 and 160 — separation appearing only at long context and growing with it, which is the signature of a **slope** change, and not an ordering artefact since the arm order alternates. **`G-E53f` registered BEFORE the re-run**: the RANK partner E14 §3 requires for `G-E53d`'s SCORE — complete separation of `k` reps against `k`, `p = 2/C(2k,k) = 0.0079` at `k=5`, `INCONSISTENT` if windows separate both ways, answering **which** arm is faster and **never by how much**. Eight planted controls, runner now **27 of 27**; **run 1 is NOT re-scored by it** (E52's precedent). Scorecard **3 right / 3 wrong**, both misses on magnitude, the speed rows still blank.

**2026-09-13 (notte, E52) — THE OCCUPANCY BAR WAS GUESSED; NOW IT IS MEASURED, AND IT IS `4.39%`, NOT `15.0%`.** E52 loads the box to six deliberate foreign levels (`L ∈ {0,1,2,3,5,8}` busy single-core processes, 3 reps, **level order ROTATED** each rep so level and time are not the same axis, plus an `L=0` drift witness opening and closing outside the rotation) and fits rate against **measured** foreign occupancy — the knob is never assumed to deliver its nominal level. **All four gates fire.** `G-E52a2` (control, purely structural after run 1): monotone, `L=0` the minimum at **4.4%**, span **16.1×** to 71.3%. `G-E52b` (drift): **CLEAN**, the bracketing `L=0` cells agree to **2.09%**. `G-E52c`: **`rate = 111.19 · (1 − 0.2622/100 · f)`, i.e. `k = 0.262% of rate per point of foreign occupancy`**, residuals within **±1.4%** across the whole fitted domain. `G-E52d`, by a rule fixed before the data (*the largest foreign occupancy whose predicted cost is at most HALF the 2.3% zero-load dispersion*): **`OCC_BAR` 15.0 → 4.39**, applied at `e44_interval.py:74`. **Consequence for E44: run 2's 3.8% foreign is UNDER the new bar, so `110.62 tok/s` stays citable; run 1's ~12.6% is far above it and stays non-citable** — which answers E44 addendum C's open question, but **only half in my favour**: the fitted cost of that load difference is **2.33 points** of the **7.50** between the two runs, so **5.2 points remain unexplained** (thermal soak is the standing suspicion and nothing measures it). **The bar is NECESSARY, NOT SUFFICIENT** — passing it means a reading is not LOAD-contaminated, not that two passing readings are comparable; the ±5% on absolute rates stays. **My predictions were 3 right, 5 WRONG**, and in one direction: I assumed light foreign load is expensive and it is not — six free hyperthreads absorb five busy processes for **3% of rate**, the linear form holds cleanly to **46.1% foreign** (I predicted a bend at ~20%), and `k` came in **below** both my §5 range (0.6–1.2) and my revised A.6 range (0.3–0.7). There is no bend; there is a **cliff** at `L=8` (rate to a third, dispersion **46.5%**), excluded by **two independent pre-registered rules** — A.5's structural `L ≤ 6` domain and §4's 6% dispersion rule. **The counterfactual is the finding:** with NEITHER exclusion the fit returns `k = 0.724` and a bar of `1.59%`, **both inside my original predicted ranges** — my guesses would have been scored CORRECT by a fit with oversubscription in it. *Registering the exclusion rules before the data made the scorecard worse for me, which is what they are for.* Run 1 is kept **UNSCORED** (its control fired: I registered `L=0 must read below 5%` without ever measuring this box's 5–7% idle foreign floor — **`G-E44b`'s exact defect, committed inside the brief written to fix `G-E44b`** — and I was also polling the log, i.e. *I was the foreign load in the experiment measuring foreign load*). The clause was **removed, not retuned**, because §2 had already made measured foreign the x-axis. E52 measures the INSTRUMENT: no rate in it is a speed result for any arm.

**2026-09-13 (notte, E44 run 2) — THE FIRST tok/s IN THIS PROGRAMME WITH A MEASURED INTERVAL ON A BOX PROVEN QUIET: `110–112 tok/s`, MEDIAN `110.62`, SPREAD `2.3%`.** Arm R128 `--carve-k 3`, `--bench 300` (mean context position **150**, not 20), on `donor_engine.exe` which is **sha256-identical to `donor_engine_e50.exe`**, i.e. the avx4 default. **But first, `G-E44b` is MALFORMED and always was** (addendum B): `Occupancy.sample()` is system-wide `GetSystemTimes` and the engine runs `--threads 6` on a 6c/12t part, so while decoding **it is about half the machine** — the guard read `10.4%` with nothing running and `42–50%` with the engine running, and **the difference was the engine**. `OCC_BAR = 15.0` is a bar on FOREIGN load, so **no state of any machine could ever have passed it**. Re-registered as **`G-E44b2`, relaxing nothing** — same arm, same 300 tokens, same 6 threads, same 15.0% bar, same 5-rep minimum — with one change to *what is measured*: foreign occupancy = system busy minus the engine process's own CPU (`GetProcessTimes` on the child handle). **Two planted controls, and the second is the one that matters**: C1 refuses a box loaded on twelve cores (`100.0%`), and **C2 requires the meter to DISCRIMINATE** — during a real rep, system above the bar, foreign below it, separated by ≥25 points, *because a metric that always returned zero would pass every box and look exactly like a correct one*. C2 fired at **system 36.8% vs foreign 3.8%, separation 33.1 points**. **`G-E44b2` FIRES.** **And the registered prediction that the band would not move was WRONG, by +7.5% (102.90 → 110.62), which is exactly what it was registered to catch**: run 1's guard read `10.4%` against run 2's `2.2%`, so run 1 carried **~12.6 points of foreign load** — and **under `G-E44b2` run 1 would have passed too**, at 12.6% foreign, citing 102.90 where run 2 cites 110.62. **Two runs, one gate, 7.5% apart, both passing: the quantity is now right and the BAR is too loose.** The implied sensitivity is ~0.85% of rate per point of foreign occupancy and **is not promoted to anything** (two runs, confounded with thermal history). What follows without it: **every band must be quoted with its guard reading and foreign median beside it**, and the bar is owed a measurement — deliberately loading the box to a sequence of foreign levels — rather than a post-hoc choice of threshold. Unarranged corroboration: E49's `avx4` fit gives **105.61 tok/s** at mean position 150 against the measured **110.62**, `+4.7%`, inside the standing ±5%. **And the spread is the other result: 2.3% across five reps**, against E43's 9–22% "on a quiet box" and E51's 8–12% over a 35-minute run — **the dispersion this programme has been budgeting for is mostly CONDITIONS, not the engine.**

**2026-09-13 (notte, E51) — `-fno-math-errno` IS A NO-OP HERE, AND THE REASON IS IN THE LIBM, NOT THE COMPILER.** `G-E51a` **PARITY HOLDS at `0.000e+00` relative** — `NATS_TOTAL 124963.9517608703` on both builds, **identical to every digit** over 12,264 positions — with the planted control firing at `1.593e-01`, **159× its `1e-03` bar**. `G-E51b` **PASS**: A1 **160/160**, controls **3/160** and **10/160**, reproducing E6 to the token for the fourth time. `G-E51c`: **NO FIT — ONE RESOLVABLE WINDOW**, and at that window (n=160, −0.2%) the two builds are **NOT separable**; no `C50` is published. **`G-E51d`: NO IMPROVEMENT** come registrato -- e la sua misura e il pezzo che serve: **l'esponenziale del softmax e `1,689 ms` di un token da `13,887 ms` a posizione media 640, cioe il `12,2%`**, da tre ripetizioni che disperdono l'**1,8%**. Quello e il TETTO dell'esperimento che la sez.6 rimanda (un `exp2` polinomiale vettorizzato), e sara meno, perche il rimpiazzo valuta comunque un polinomio per elemento. Il confronto fra build su `S` **non si usa**: il braccio trattato disperde l'**83,6%** (2,890/1,660/1,502) contro l'1,8% del base -- *un numero che oscilla dell'84% non si confronta con uno che oscilla del 2%*. Il totale della build e51 e **+1,12% piu LENTO**, la direzione che A.2 prevede una volta capito che il flag aggiunge un frame invece di togliere lavoro, ma e dentro il rumore e non viene rivendicato. **The mechanism, read out of the object code:** the flag did everything brief §1 said it would do to the CALLER — the two `vcvt`s, the per-element `vzeroupper` and the reloaded loop bound leave the loop, which unrolls 4×, and the binaries genuinely differ (**8 `exp` / 0 `expf`** becomes **6 `exp` / 49 `expf`**). Then the callee: `expf:  cvtss2sd ; callq <exp> ; cvtsd2ss ; retq`. **`expf` on this toolchain IS `(float)exp((double)x)` written out.** The promotion to double was **relocated into the callee, not removed**, with a call frame added on top — which is also why the two builds agree bit-for-bit: they compute the same values in the same order. **THE LAW: an improvement read on one side of a call can be an accounting move. The cost is not the caller's assembly, it is the caller's plus the callee's** — cousin of Phase 61 (compute-bound microbench does not compose to a memory-bound engine) and of the byte-convention law. **Runner defect, fixed:** `fit()` raised on a single judged window, so `G-E51c` printed its table and then **no verdict at all**, and the attribution phase never ran; `g_e51c_summary` now handles 0, 1 and ≥2 resolvable windows, three new self-tests plant all three, **20 of 20 fire**, and the verdict was **re-scored from run 1's own 50 cells** by `e51c_rescore.py` with **no cell re-measured** (E50 B.8's treatment). **A diagnostic that is NOT promoted to a gate** (A.4): the two arms' levels **step down together at the same repetition** in the long windows and stay down (1280: 84.33 → 75.89 on base, 83.09 → 75.96 on e51), so the dispersion that made four windows unresolvable is a **shared drift under sustained load**, not per-measurement noise — the paired ratios are −0.2%, −0.1%, −0.2%, +0.0%. Registering that rule needs its own gate and its own control; using it here would be `G-E49c` with the sign flipped. **And a units note that touches E49:** every `|ΔBPB|` this programme derives from `NATS_TOTAL` rests on an **assumed 5.0 BPB** — `donor_engine.c:1625` prints no byte total, and solving E49's stored pair backwards returns `4.999999`, the constant it was multiplied by. Nothing is withdrawn (the relative agreement is load-bearing and scale-free) but **the scored byte total is owed in the ids sidecar**. What E51 bought despite a null treatment: it **priced the deferred experiment upward** — both builds still execute the **entire double-precision `exp` routine, once per element**, so only replacing the routine removes it.

**Last updated: 2026-09-13 (notte, E49 addendum C) — I WITHDRAW `G-E49c`: THE HEADLINE IS NOT RESTATED, AND I MIS-SPECIFIED THE GATE.** `G-E49c` read the two kernels' medians at `NTOK = 40` against a ±5% band and returned "restated". **The dispersions at that window, printed by the same runner two lines above its own verdict, are 21.0% and 7.9%**, and the five-rep ranges **OVERLAP**: `serial`'s best repetition is **121.38 tok/s** and `avx4`'s median is **121.65**. The arms are not distinguishable at the window the gate was registered on, nor at n=160; they separate cleanly only from **n=640**. See `briefs/BRIEF_E45_DOES_SPEED_DEPEND_ON_THE_WEIGHTS.md` (addenda A-F) and `briefs/BRIEF_E46_WHAT_DOES_A_TOKEN_COST_AT_A_REAL_CONTEXT.md` (addenda A-B).  **Last updated: 2026-09-13 (late) — THE BUNDLE IS PACKED AND THE TRAINER'S OWN CPU SMOKE KILLED SESSION 2 AT STARTUP, WHICH IS THE SAME ROOT CAUSE A FOURTH TIME.** `h1_qat.py` had never been run end to end on the real bundle path. The bundle was fine; **the check asked the wrong question.** At `k = E` the carve is bit-identical under **either** the HARD gate for any router **or** the SOFT gate *only at a zero router* (uniform `p`, so `k·p/Σ_sel p = 1` exactly) — and half of H1's apparatus was written while the router happened always to be zero, so the soft form looked unconditional. A `--resume` starts from a **trained** router and fails by construction. That is now **four defects from one cause**: `G-H1` scored soft against hard; `G-H1e` pitted a soft router against a hard STATIC; the router smoke did the same for 11.7 h on the comparison that chose what to ship; and `G-H1a`. **Repaired with a control that fails on purpose** — both forms are checked and both must fire, and `h1_selftest.py` gained **T8** on a toy with a deliberately non-zero router, where the WRONG form is required to FAIL first (`T8a` max|d| **3.011e+00**) before `T8`/`T8b` (both **0.0e+00**) are trusted; T1–T8 all fire. **THE RESUME IS REAL, and this is the control the whole two-session design rests on and nothing had ever run it**: session 1 fresh step-0 BPB **1.442147**, after its 2 steps **1.431063**, session 2 `--resume` step-0 **1.431063** — a silent restart would have read 1.442147. **Three smaller defects, all found by running the program**: a label that lied (`"fp16, GPU"` printed beside a CPU fp32 number); **a regression introduced ~40 minutes earlier while fixing it** (`prec` defined in `main()`, referenced in `save()` — `NameError` at the **first periodic checkpoint**, which on the T4 destroys the whole session's output at the 250-step save while the run looks healthy, and `py_compile`, `ast.parse` and the self-test all passed); and a **gate value nobody could reproduce** (`carve_is_live` read 2.082e+01 / 2.575e+01 / 2.231e+01 on three runs of the same bundle — an unseeded `torch.randn` probe whose number is **stored in the record**; seeded now). Torch's `use_cache` and `requires_grad` warnings are **benign** — the second comes from the *eval* pass under `no_grad`, and `G-H1c` demonstrates training is unaffected by moving masters at **both** depth extremes, `L03.gate` and `L24.down`. **STATE: `_h1_bundle/` is 419.3 MB with all four packer gates FIRED** — self-test T1–T8, `G-H1d`, the router setting **read from** `h1_router_select.json` and never typed, and the null control's decomposition at experts **+0.000000000000** / router **+0.000000000000**. `RUN.md` was also repaired: its template crashed on a literal `{0,1}` read as a format field, it still quoted addendum E's **retired** "11.7 h / 2.27%" numbers (now the corrected 2 of 8 and **+2.67%**, with the retraction stated in the handover itself), and it gained a "what the first 30 seconds should print" section naming both `G-H1a` lines and the benign warnings. **It is now the user's move: two T4 sessions, commands in `COMMUNICATION.md`.** See `briefs/BRIEF_H1_THE_CARVE_TRAINED_NOT_APPLIED.md` addendum I.  **Last updated: 2026-09-13 (evening) — THE CORRECTED GRID SHIPS THE SAME SETTING, AND BOTH PREDICTIONS I REGISTERED FOR IT ARE WRONG.** The full 12×8 grid re-ran in **4542 s (1,26 h)** hard-gated, with the bit-exactness-asserted quantisation cache and all 96 trained routers saved. `h1_router_select.py` applied addendum D.3's rule verbatim to the corrected table and shipped **`--router-lr 0.0003 --aux 0.01`** — the same setting, the same **2 of 8** layers `[3, 24]`, the same rule-4 shortfall, and **0 of 96 cells changed their beats-STATIC verdict**. That is the branch §G.5 registered as the uncomfortable outcome: gate form was not load-bearing in the smoke, and addendum E's selection returns on its own merits. **Prediction 1 falsified** — no extra settings beat STATIC; the other eight still win 0 of 8. **Prediction 2 falsified in direction, and the mechanism with it**: the gate-form gap `(soft−hard)/hard` is governed by **lr**, not `aux` — ≤0,4% in magnitude at lr ≤ 1e-3, then **+7,53% / +13,49% / +18,69%** at lr 1e-2 for aux 0 / 0,01 / 0,1, i.e. it **grows** with `aux`, the opposite of what I wrote. The reason is reusable: the soft gate's distortion is set by the spread of `p` **inside the selected k**, while `occ_max` counts how many *tokens* share a group — and `aux` flattens token-level load precisely **by** making the router discriminate more sharply per token. **I used `occ_max` as a proxy for a quantity it does not proxy.** This also scopes addendum F honestly: the **+0,801377 BPB** gate-form cost was measured with E37's peaked post-hoc router, whereas a router trained here at lr 3e-4 gives **under 0,4%** — different routers, both numbers right, and `engine.c` ships the hard gate either way. **A THIRD defect turned up and it was not a gate**: the original grid ran as **two processes with different `--layers`**, and `capture_inputs` forwards through the model with the carved FFNs **already installed** — so the L6-24 process captured with **layer 3 uncarved**, against a model H1 will never run. Layer 3 reproduces to every printed digit across the two processes (power 0,130669, STATIC 0,108585, init 0,124098), which is a free control: the harness is deterministic, so every downstream difference is **the carve composing**, not noise. Total STATIC error mass **1,499502 → 1,388415 (−7,41%)**. §E.4's depth concentration survives with its magnitude trimmed — layer 24 alone **41,1% → 38,7%**, layers 21+24 **60,3% → 58,2%**, relative damage still falling monotonically with depth (STATIC/power 0,8310 at L3 → 0,6072 at L24). **General rule kept: a grid split across processes is one experiment only if every process installs the same model.** **Addendum D.4's narrowing is vindicated after the fact** — measured correctly, all eight settings it dropped win **0 of 8** (mean ratios 1,0939 to 1,1931); retiring it was still right, because a decision made on void evidence is void even when it lands on the right answer, but it cost no information. **Re-registered on a valid instrument, before the T4 run: `G-H1e` is expected to FAIL** (2 of 8 layers, **+2,67%** aggregate error mass against STATIC; the only setting better in aggregate is `lr=3e-4,aux=0` at **−2,02%** and rule 1 excludes it at mean `occ_max` 0,7720, collapsed to 1,000 on the very layer it wins). **`G-H1` — the experts, `trained-8L < applied-8L = 1,096636`, hard gate — is untouched and remains the primary gate.** What the 1,26 h bought, flatly: **the gate repair changed no decision**; it bought a selection resting on a matched instrument, the split-process defect, 96 saved routers so nothing here needs retraining to be re-scored, and two falsified predictions including a mechanism error I would otherwise still believe. See `briefs/BRIEF_H1_THE_CARVE_TRAINED_NOT_APPLIED.md` addendum H.  **Last updated: 2026-09-13 (later still) — THE SAME CONFOUND WAS IN THE ROUTER SMOKE, SO THE SETTING H1 WAS ABOUT TO SHIP IS RETIRED AND THE FULL GRID IS RE-RUN FOR 1.1 HOURS INSTEAD OF 33.** Having priced the soft gate at +0.801377 BPB, the obvious next question was what else shared the defect — and `h1_router_smoke.py` never set `hard_gate` at all. `set_static` returns gates of **exactly 1** whatever `hard_gate` says (it short-circuits on `_static`), so every cell of addenda D and E compared a **soft router against a hard STATIC**. It was never a measurement of selection. **And the artefact is not a constant offset: it interacts with the parameter being chosen.** The soft gate's damage grows with router peakedness and `aux` exists precisely to flatten the router, so the `aux=0` cells (`occ_max` 0.82–1.00) were penalised hardest and the `aux=0.01` cells (`occ_max` 0.12–0.16) barely at all — i.e. the balanced-vs-collapsed comparison that decided the shipped setting is exactly the one the artefact corrupts. **Addendum G retires** addendum D.1's layer-3 table, **addendum D.4's grid narrowing** (eight settings dropped on distorted numbers), addendum E.1's eight-layer table with its shipped `--router-lr 3e-4 --aux 0.01` and its "2 of 8 layers" shortfall, and addendum E's registered expectation that `G-H1e` fails — that prediction was **derived from** the void numbers, and a prediction inherited from a void instrument is not a prediction. **What survives is load-bearing and worth naming**: the planted control (a gate-independent wiring check, router grad 1.361e+00/3.983e-01); `init` and `denom`, both measured at a **zero** router where soft and hard coincide exactly for every `k` — now pinned by an assertion in the runner rather than by the loop's habit; the **selection RULE of addendum D.3**, which speaks of eligibility and layers-won and says nothing about gates, re-applied **unchanged** to the new table by `h1_router_select.py`, so the rule still precedes the data; and §E.4's structural reading that the carve's damage concentrates deep (layer 24 alone = 41.1% of total error mass, layers 21+24 = 60.3%), which is STATIC-versus-uncarved-power and hard on both sides. **The re-run is affordable because of a 30× that costs nothing**: the smoke freezes the experts, yet `_quant` re-ran `r3_actsearch` every step — a 10-point threshold grid over three `[8960,1536]` tensors, ~3.3 G element-ops per forward, **92% of the file's cost** — when with frozen experts `ste()` is a constant. Measured on the real shape: **4.164 → 0.138 s/step**, and the cached tensors are `torch.equal` to what `_quant` returns; the run **asserts** that per layer and refuses to start otherwise, so speed is not traded against correctness on a promise. The full 12-setting × 8-layer grid therefore costs **~1.1 h against 33 h**, which means the re-run does **not** inherit the narrowing — every setting the first pass discarded on distorted evidence is measured again, correctly gated, and the narrowing objection disappears instead of being argued about. Two further repairs, both about the same class of mistake: the **trained routers are now saved** (`*_routers.npz`), because the first version threw them away and that is why a mis-gated *evaluation* forced a full *retrain* — a measurement that cannot be re-scored is a measurement that must be re-run, and that is a harness defect, not bad luck; and the runner's own "GO. Best setting" headline now prints **THIS LINE IS NOT THE DECISION** and points at `h1_router_select.py`, the second time in two days a script's printed verdict outlived the spec it was written against. **Registered before the numbers exist**: more settings will beat STATIC than before (the handicap is removed from the router arm and from nothing else), and `aux=0` will gain more than `aux=0.01` because it was penalised more — but **rule 1 still excludes collapsed routers on `occ_max`, unchanged**, for the reason §D.2 gave: a collapsed router is a STATIC router with extra steps. If the new table instead looks like the old one, gate form was not load-bearing in the smoke and addendum E's selection returns on its own merits — stated here so that outcome is available rather than embarrassing. See `briefs/BRIEF_H1_THE_CARVE_TRAINED_NOT_APPLIED.md` addendum G.  **Last updated: 2026-09-13 (later) — THE NULL CONTROL PRICED THE GATE FORM AT +0.80 BPB, AND TWO OF H1'S REGISTERED GATES WERE MEASURING IT INSTEAD OF TRAINING.** `h1_nullbundle.py` builds the donor's own experts plus E37's own routers with **zero training** — by construction the model `applied-8L` measured. Through `h1_eval.py` its decomposition must be zeros and **is**: experts +0.000000, router +0.000000, residual 0.0e+00, `arm-E` and `arm-ER` both reproducing **1.096636** exactly. The third term is the finding: **the renormalised soft gate costs +0.801377 BPB BY ITSELF**, ~160σ, the largest single effect in H1's apparatus — gates average 1 but are not each 1, so a peaked router amplifies a dominant group by `k·p_max/Σ_sel p` and the experts were never trained to expect it. **Consequence 1: `G-H1` was scoring a SOFT-gated trained model against a HARD-gated control**, charging H1 ~0.80 BPB of gate form before training counted — and `engine.c` runs the HARD gate, so the soft number measures an object that cannot ship. `G-H1` is now scored HARD; training stays soft because a hard gate has no router gradient, and the train/eval gap is a measured term of the decomposition rather than an assumption. **Consequence 2, worse: `G-H1e` could not isolate routing at all** — `STATIC` always had gates exactly 1, so it pitted a soft router against a hard fixed selection and read **5.564279 vs 3.435791 nats/token, "FAILS", with zero training**. Both arms are now HARD; only the selection differs. **This is a legitimate pre-run change and the brief argues it explicitly: no trained weights exist, the evidence is a NULL bundle whose decomposition is required to be zeros and is, the change comes from a property of the deliverable, it makes one gate easier and the other harder, and `applied-8L` = 1.096636 does not move.** The pre-repair run is kept as `h1_eval_NULLCONTROL_softgate_prerepair.json`. (Detail: `briefs/BRIEF_H1_THE_CARVE_TRAINED_NOT_APPLIED.md` addendum F.)  — previously: 2026-09-13 — H1'S CPU HALF IS COMPLETE AND THE ROUTER SETTING IS CHOSEN BY A RULE REGISTERED BEFORE THE DATA, WITH ITS SHORTFALL CLAUSE FIRING.** All eight layers of the router smoke landed (11.7 h CPU, planted control firing in both runs). `h1_router_select.py` executes addendum D.3's five clauses and picks nothing itself: **`--router-lr 3e-4 --aux 0.01`**, eligible at mean `occ_max` 0.3014, beating a STATIC selection on **2 of 8 layers** — below the majority of 5, so **rule 4 fires and the setting ships with the shortfall on the record**. Rule 5 does not fire, so H1 launches. **The runner's own printed headline (`--aux 0`) is SUPERSEDED** — it ranks by layers-won alone, never saw layer 3, and broke a four-way tie by list order: the same trap as E43's stale `VOID` line, a script's verdict outliving its specification. **And the rank and score metrics disagree (E14 §3): the shipped setting is +2.27% WORSE than STATIC in total error mass, while the only setting better in aggregate (−1.43%) is the one that collapses (`occ_max` 0.998 on layer 24). So this smoke does NOT establish that a trained router beats a static one in this regime**, and addendum E registers the expectation that `G-H1e` FAILS. H1's primary gate `G-H1` is about the EXPERTS and stands. **New and unasked-for: the carve's damage is nowhere near evenly spread** — layer 24 alone carries **41.1%** of total carve error and layers 21+24 carry **60.3%**, while relative damage FALLS with depth (0.89 at layer 9 → 0.63 at layer 24); `H1_LAYERS` is an even spread over a very uneven target, logged and NOT acted on because moving it would break comparability with the `applied-8L` anchor. **Three defects caught before a single GPU hour**: h1_qat had NO FFN resume path at all (an H1 bundle in `--factors` would have installed zero attention organs and restarted the FFN on the raw donor — now `--resume`, and `build_qo` refuses to install nothing); `_quant`'s cache keys on autograd `_version`, which `.data.copy_` deliberately does not bump, so a resumed module kept serving the weights it was told to replace (self-test T7, `invalidate()`); and `h1_eval`'s `G-H1a` asked for a bit-identity that cannot hold under a soft gate with a fitted router — caught by the NULL-CONTROL bundle on its first use, which is exactly what it is for. (Detail: `briefs/BRIEF_H1_THE_CARVE_TRAINED_NOT_APPLIED.md` addendum E; `s1/h1_router_select.py`, `s1/h1_eval.py`, `s1/h1_pack.py`.)  — previously: 2026-09-12 (sera) — **E43's SPEED HALF IS CLOSED WITH NO RESULT, PERMANENTLY — and what came out of the attempt is a fact about the INSTRUMENT, not about vocabularies.** The user emptied the machine and the one registered attempt (addendum A, re-registered verbatim in addendum C) was spent: two sessions, forward and reversed, 5 of 5 reps surviving the box bar in each, boxes at 1.0–13.3% busy — the quietest this programme has measured. **`G-E43A2` FIRES** — both orders put the peak at `V8192` and both put the inherited `V=32768` above it, cells 1.0939 and 1.1095. **`G-E43A1` FAILS** — the control arm's own within-session spread reads **22.3% against the registered 13.5% bar** on the reversed session (forward passed at 8.9%). **So E43 has no speed half: the cell, the name `VOCABULARY-IS-A-SMALL-LEVER` and `CELL 2` may not be cited anywhere, ever.** The outlier that broke it is not removable — rep 5 was NOT a fast rep (relative rate 1.0153, below rep 2's), rule 3 drops reps by box occupancy and never by value, and removing it would be the post-hoc adjustment addendum C had just been written to prevent. **The draw was unkind and that is not a defence**: the control cell was worst of 20 reversed and 5th best forward, so anchoring the gate anywhere else would have passed it — a floor that only holds on a kind draw is not a floor. **⚠ AND THE CORRECTION THAT IS OWED, BIGGER THAN E43: `112,73 tok/s` DOES NOT CARRY ITS FOUR SIGNIFICANT FIGURES.** On a genuinely idle box this engine's absolute tok/s disperses **9–22%** and it is INTRINSIC: 40 cells across the two sessions, median spread 10.9% and 9.2%, 4 of 20 over 13.5% in each — the same magnitude E40 saw on a BUSIER box, so emptying the machine did not shrink it. Page-cache residency, never controlled by E39/E40/E43, stays a HYPOTHESIS: weakened as an explanation of the outlier, untouched as an explanation of the level. Il profilo `MINRES` (definito nel brief PRIMA del run: media sulle posizioni di calibrazione di `||out-in||/||in||`) **non e monotono**: 19.05 al layer 0, minimo 0.2836 al 15, e **risale a 0.7618 al 27** -- percio "togli gli ultimi `n`", la mossa di default, rimuove alcuni dei blocchi PIU attivi. Due predizioni su cinque mancate e una mancata di 4x in magnitudine, entrambe le mancate informative e trovabili solo perche i numeri erano depositati prima. **Niente timing, niente esportato: 6.79 tok/s e i 4.70 tok/s di `T10` restano INTATTI.** **H0 resta l'unico ramo aperto del donor, ed e senza router e senza carve per costruzione, quindi ne E23 ne E24 ne E27 lo toccano.**)** Previously: (**E24 `DEPTH-RECOVERS`: il `ROUTER-COSTS` di E23 era un artefatto della PROFONDITA, e alla profondita che il budget permetteva da sempre la configurazione composta si RIPRENDE -- con un router vero, addebitato ed ESEGUITO.** (`probes/E24_THE_DEPTH_THE_BUDGET_PERMITS.md`, brief `b78ce4d` pushato prima che il runner esistesse; nove bracci, `VOID: none`, 3842 s, zero timing.) **`G-U1` non riproduce E23 dentro `1e-9`: lo riproduce a `abs diff 0.0` su ENTRAMBE le ancore** (`QO512+V52-LINEAR` `1.2014771810176486`/9/102 e `QO512+V52-ORACLE` `1.0050386831300866`/15/126), il che dimostra inerte sia il refactor di `fit_routers` in `fit_routers_multi` sia la fattorizzazione low-rank calcolata UNA volta invece di otto. **LA CURVA: `k=133` 102 -> `k=139` 105 -> `k=148` 110 -> `k=156` 118 token teacher-forced, monotona su entrambe le metriche.** **`K156-LINEAR` -- `1.0590 G` attivi, DENTRO `0.982-1.060 G` di E18 sez.31, col router da `11.0 M` addebitato e girato -- legge `118/160`, un token sotto CHEAPER: e la PRIMA configurazione di questo programma che sta nel budget, non usa NESSUN oracolo, ed e COMPARABLE sull'ordinamento. La pendenza vera qui e ~1.8 token per punto di attivazione, circa cinque volte quella media: il danno del carve NON e lineare nella profondita, ACCELERA scendendo -- cioe l'errore sta nella direzione che peggiora il trasferimento, non lo migliora.** **E IL CONTRAPPESO, che non va sepolto: questo e il conteggio ASSOLUTO del donor da 1.5 B contro un budget assoluto, e la ricetta NON si trasferisce alla forma dell'obiettivo come FRAZIONE.** Trasposta a `T10` sulla linea MISURATA di E25 (49.9 G pesi/s), `QO512+K156` fa `6.14 G` = **~8.1 tok/s, non 50**; e al contrario, a `T10` il budget da 50 tok/s lascia alla FFN **`k ~ 2...8` di 256 = 0.8%-3.2% di attivazione** (pavimento non-FFN `0.9899 G` a `r=512`, `0.7885 G` a `r=256`; con `q/o` denso o a `r=D/3` e IMPOSSIBILE), dove E24 ha misurato la qualita al **51.9%-60.9%** e l'ha vista SALIRE con la profondita. **Quindi la leva che apre la forma dell'obiettivo e quella che abbassa il PAVIMENTO non-FFN -- rango su `q/o`, poi la testa -- non piu profondita di FFN**, ed e l'item aperto di E21 sez.8 (`r/D=1/3` validato solo a `D=1536`) e di E22 sez.8. **E ogni tok/s qui e CONDIZIONATO**: assume l'invariante di throughput addebitato di E25 anche per un braccio SCOLPITO, che e esattamente cio che misura E26 parte B -- **e E26 parte B non ha ancora un record valido** (primo tentativo su macchina contesa, VOID). **Free-running ancora AT-FLOOR (15/160 contro pavimento 12): si e mosso solo l'ordinamento.** **Niente timing, niente esportato: 6.79 tok/s e i 4.70 tok/s di `T10` restano INTATTI.**)** Previously: (**E25 `RANK-PAYS-WHAT-IT-WEIGHS`: `engine.c` HA un matvec fattorizzato, e girato contro PyTorch, e alla forma dell'OBIETTIVO la leva del rango rende esattamente i pesi che toglie.** (`probes/E25_WHAT_THE_RANK_COSTS.md`, brief `e506c7b` pushato prima che il runner girasse.) **L'item dovuto da E21 sez.8, ripetuto da E22 sez.8, E23 sez.9 e dalla proposta T4 sez.6, e SALDATO**: E21 ed E22 misuravano il taglio di rango calcolando `A.B` e installando una matrice DENSA -- il modo giusto di prezzarne la QUALITA e nessun modo di prezzarne il COSTO -- quindi ogni conseguenza del rango sui tok/s era ARITMETICA. `donor_engine.c` guadagna una specie `mat_t` fattorizzata (`y = A(s . (B x)) + b`, due chiamate agli STESSI kernel, intermedio di taglia `r`) e **`quant == 3`, un contenitore TAGGATO** dove ogni matrice porta il proprio `int32` kind: senza di quello le configurazioni misurate qui non erano nemmeno ESPRIMIBILI (E21/E22/E23 lasciano `k/v` in **fp32** mentre tagliano `q/o`, e un flag `quant` globale non lo sa dire) -- **fino a oggi ogni risultato di rango era un numero PyTorch senza artefatto eseguibile dietro.** **`G-E25P` SCATTA: l2 relativo peggiore `6.445e-04` contro una barra di `2e-3`, accordo top-1 `1.0000` su 10/10**, sul `QO512-TB` di E22 -- lo stato da cui H0 parte -- contro un riferimento PyTorch che monta `h0_qat.TernaryLowRank`, lo stesso modulo che il T4 allena. **`G-E25a`** tiene fermi i percorsi vecchi: motore patchato e non patchato danno logit BIT-IDENTICI. **E il CONTROLLO PIANTATO e il cuore della parte B**: a `r = D/2` una proiezione quadrata muove ESATTAMENTE gli stessi pesi (`2.D.(D/2) = D^2`), quindi quello che perde e il costo PROPRIO del percorso fattorizzato -- e legge **`-0.43%` a `T10` e `-2.46%` a `S15`, con dispersioni 7.3% e 6.4%: non risolvibile da zero.** Le 56/96 regioni OpenMP in piu per token costano meno di quanto questa macchina sappia misurare. **Alla forma dell'obiettivo (`T10`, 10.60 G attivi, le dimensioni dell'"es 10B") il rango 512 su `q/o` legge `5.36` contro `4.70` tok/s = `+14.12%`, contro una predizione a byte di `+12.86%`: l'alternativa registrata ("non batte del 8%") NON scatta.** **E l'invariante vale piu di ogni rapporto: quattro bracci `T10` i cui ritmi differiscono del 15% consegnano throughput addebitato dentro una banda dell'1.54% (49.59-50.36 G pesi/s)** -- il motore converte pesi attivi in tempo a un ritmo che NON guarda come sono disposti, quindi il rango compra velocita attraverso UN solo meccanismo, meno pesi, ed e per questo che `2.D.r` e l'addebito giusto. **La linea di budget di E18 sez.31 (`0.982-1.060 G`/token per 50 tok/s) mappa, al throughput MISURATO di `T10`, su 47.0-50.7 tok/s: costruita su forme piu piccole, abbraccia 50 alla forma dell'obiettivo, misurata per la prima volta.** **E la distanza, misurata invece che derivata: `T10` gira a 4.70 tok/s, l'obiettivo e 50, quindi 10.6x -- e il rango su `q/o` la porta a 9.3x.** Il rango su `q/o` vale `1.2 G` dei `9.6 G` che devono uscire; **la FFN e `8.45 G` di `T10`, nove decimi del problema che resta**, ed e esattamente dove puntano il carve di E19 e il router di E23/E24 -- **E24 e ancora da girare.** Due predizioni prese, una parziale, due mancate (la 3 SOPRA la banda, nella direzione This is the map. Every row names the artefact that holds the detail; nothing here is a claim that
is not written up somewhere with its controls and its pre-registration.

## 0. Where the goal actually stands

| | status |
|---|---|
| **A trained LLM, FAITHFUL and FAST, both at once** | ❌ **NO.** E57 is the first experiment to put SPEED and QUALITY of REAL trained weights in one gated table; the quality readings themselves are **E17's, replicated to the token** (addendum B — §3's "never been scored" was false). Six real Qwen2.5 arms, 90 timed cells, quality scored by E51's scorer against E6's HuggingFace references with all three E6 controls reproducing (`G-E57a` fires). **FAITHFUL and slow:** `05b_f32` 160/160 at **19.09 tok/s**, `15b_f32` 160/160 at **6.25**. **FAST and broken:** `05b_tqh` **83.41** at 3/160, `05b_tq` **43.69** at 3/160, `15b_tqh` **29.18** at 10/160, `15b_tq` **19.15** at 12/160. The fastest faithful arm is **38% of the 50 tok/s bar**; the fastest arm is 4.4× faster and emits 3 correct tokens in 160. **Post-hoc ternarisation of the BODY — not the head, which was my registered prediction — diverges at token 0.** Every tok/s this programme has published sits on the broken side of that table, on synthetic weights. The empty cell is **ternary BY TRAINING at donor scale**, which has never been built. (`briefs/BRIEF_E57_...md` addendum A) |
| **The rung between fp32 and ternary** | ✅ **BUILT, AND IT IS THE FIRST FAITHFUL ARM OVER THE BAR.** One byte per weight — never exported, never timed, never scored before E60 — reads **63.87 tok/s at 0.5 B (ABOVE 50)** and **21.39 at 1.5 B**, ×3.35/×3.41 over fp32, for **+0.000066 / +0.001252 BPB** (0.013 / 0.250 σ_seed) against packed's +3.66 / +2.71 above chance. Needed **no kernel**: `matvec_sel`'s `quant==1` branch is already a general int8×fp32 dot; `quant=5` exists only so an int8 file and a ternary file do not print the same `CONFIG` line. Greedy reads 145/160 and 129/160 — near-ties tipping, not damage: the BPB says indistinguishable and §2.1 shows the count is a tie-breaking measure, not a fidelity one. **And the byte ladder is not a bandwidth ladder: 84–85% of the fp32 arm's GB/s at 1 B, 57–59% at 0.5 B** — **corrected by E61 to 90% at 1 B** once every kernel runs four accumulator chains, and the rates to **68.52 / 22.56 tok/s**. (`probes/E60_...md`) |
| **The accumulator chain on the byte that works** | ❌ **PAID, AND IT WAS WORTH 5%, NOT 19%.** `matvec_sel`'s third weight branch — the int8 rung — was the last one still running a single FMA chain (E8 §9 item 3, carried unpaid through E9/E10/E11/E13/E60). Breaking it reads **×1.0507 at 0.5 B (REFUTED against the 1.06 floor)** and **×1.0662 at 1.5 B (UNDECIDED, paired CI 0.9936–1.1768)** against a derived ×1.12–1.22. **E61's own planted positive is what killed E61's mechanism:** `PACKED` sits at **90.5% of its single-chain ceiling** — the same place the int8 branch sat — and gained **×1.2745**, so *fraction-of-chain-ceiling predicts nothing*. The binder is the byte stream: **1 B/weight tops out near 34–35 GB/s** whatever the kernel does. Kept anyway — free, `--mvacc 1` is sha256-identical to E60, and the 4-chain arm is **100.0000% top-1 over 12,264 positions** at dBPB ~3e-09. Null control **1.0947** at 19.57% foreign: owed a clean repeat. (`probes/E61_...md`) |
| **Does the one-byte rung survive scale?** | ⚠ **NOT REFUTED AT 3 B, AND NO LONGER EXTRAPOLABLE.** E62 measured the third scale: **dBPB(3 B) = 0.000579 = 0.116 σ_seed**, inside the `AT-MOST-LINEAR` band, so the `COST-EXPLODES` reading that would have voided §60.4 is dead. But damage is **not monotone in N** (1.5 B is the worst of three, 2.16× the 3 B), the RANK partner breaks, and the score therefore carries **no trend claim**. The 10 B fidelity cell is now the only thing that can settle it. `probes/E62_DOES_THE_ONE_BYTE_RUNG_SURVIVE_SCALE.md` |
| **Can a 10 B model be carved AND stored at one byte per weight?** | ✅ **YES, AND THE ARITHMETIC IS EXACT.** E63 Part A built the path the engine had refused: inert on five existing artefacts, **100.0000% top-1** against packed carve, worst max |d| 1.444e-05. The 10 B int8 artefact is 10,015,507,256 B.
